mapping[\"예산\"] = mapping[\"예산\"].replace(\",\", \"\")\n\n    return mapping\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text: str, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> str:\n    def repl(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        if key in mapping:\n            return mapping[key]\n        return m.group(0)  # leave untouched if unknown\n\n    return PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n\n\n_YM_RE inclusive month span from text inside parentheses.\n\n    Examples:\n      '2025.01 ~ 2025.03' -> 3\n      '2026-08 ~ 2026-10' -> 3\n      '2027-01' -> 1\n    \"\"\"\n    raw = date_text.strip()\n         return None\n        sy, sm = start\n        ey, em = end\n        return (ey - sy) * 12 + (em - sm) + 1\n\n    single = _parse_year_month(raw)\n    if single:\n        return 1\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef append_phase_months_if_needed(text: str) -> str:\n    # Only apply to the phase lines.\n    if not (text.startswith(\"1단계:\") or text.startswith(\"2단계:\") or text.startswith(\"3단계:\")):\n        return text\n\n    # Avoid double-appending if script is run twice.\n    if re.search(r\"\\(\\d+\\s*개월\\)\", text):\n        return text\n\n    # Find the first parenthesized date part.\n    m = re.search(r\"\\(([^)]*)\\)\", text)\n    if not m:\n        return text\n\n    span = month_span_from_text(m.group(1))\n    if span is None:\n        return text\n\n inclusive month span from text inside parentheses.\n\n    Examples:\n      '2025.01 ~ 2025.03' -> 3\n      '2026-08 ~ 2026-10' -> 3\n      '2027-01' -> 1\n    \"\"\"\n    raw = date_text.strip()\n
